REPRESENTED BY ITS MANAGING DIRECTOR
…RESPONDENTS
(BY SRIYUTHS. GANGADHAR J.M, AAG AND
 RAMESH B. CHIGARI, AGA FOR R1 TO R3;
 MALLIKARJUNSWAMY B. HIREMATH, ADVOCATE FOR R4)
 THIS WP IS FILED UNDER ARTICLES 226 AND 227 OF THE
CONSTITUTION OF INDIA, PRAYING TO, A. ISSUE A WRIT OR
ORDER OF THE APPROPRIATE NATURE TO DECLARING THAT THE

 - 242 -
 HC-KAR
NC: 2025:KHC-D:13210
WP No. 107044 of 2025
PETITIONERS ARE THE MEMBERS OF THE RESPONDENT NO.4-
SOCIETY AS PER RESOLUTION DATED 28.07.2025 PRODUCED
VIDE ANNEXURE-B. B. ISSUE A WRIT IN THE NATURE OF
MANDAMUS DIRECTING THAT THE PERSONS WHERE SHARE
CAPITAL HAS BEEN PAID AND ACCEPTED AS PER THE BOARD
RESOLUTION DATED 28.07.2025 PRODUCED AT ANNEXURE-B,
BE INCLUDED IN THE ADDITIONAL LIST OF ELIGIBLE VOTERS
ISSUED BY THE RESPONDENT NO.4 ON 12.09.2025 (VIDE
ANNEXURE-A) FOR THE ELECTIONS TO GOVERNING COUNCIL
OF THE RESPONDENT NO.2- SOCIETY SCHEDULED TO BE HELD
ON 28.09.2025 AND ETC.,
 THIS PETITION, COMING ON FOR ORDERS THIS DAY,
ORDER WAS MADE THEREIN AS UNDER:
ORAL ORDER
(PER: THE HON'BLE MR. JUSTICE SURAJ GOVINDARAJ)
1. Learned AGA accepts notice for respondent Nos. 1, 2,
and 3. Sri Mallikarjanswamy Hiremath, learned
counsel is directed to accept notice for respondent No.
4.
2. The petitioners are before this Court seeking for the
following reliefs:
a. issue a Writ or order of the appropriate nature to
declaring that the Petitioners are the members of the
Respondent No.4-Society as per resolution dated:-
28.07.2025 produced vide Annexure-B,

 - 243 -
 HC-KAR
NC: 2025:KHC-D:13210
WP No. 107044 of 2025
b. issue a writ in the nature of mandamus directing
that the persons where share capital has been paid and
accepted as per the Board resolution dated 28.07.2025
produced at Annexure-B, be included in the Additional
List of Eligible Voters issued by the Respondent No.4 on
12.09.2025 (vide Annexure-A) for the elections to
Governing Council of the Respondent No.2- Society
scheduled to be held on 28.09.2025; and
c. Grant such other reliefs deemed fit in the
circumstances of the case and in the interest of justice
and equity.
3. The essential relief sought in the present petition is a
declaration that the petitioners are members of
respondent No. 4 society, in terms of the resolution
dated 28.07.2025. However, there is no order on
record admitting the petitioners to the membership of
respondent No. 4 society.
4. The petitioners have filed their applications for
membership on 01.07.2025. In terms of Section 16(4)
of the Karnataka Co-operative Societies Act, 1959 (for
short, "the Act") the petitioners have an alternative
efficacious remedy under Section 105A of the Act, the
question of the petitioners seeking a declaration that

 - 244 -
 HC-KAR
NC: 2025:KHC-D:13210
WP No. 107044 of 2025
they are members of respondent No. 4 society does
not arise.
5. Accordingly, the present petition is premature and
stands dismissed, reserving liberty to the petitioners
to approach the appropriate authority depending on
the orders passed under sub-section (4) of Section 16
of the Act.
Sd/-
(SURAJ GOVINDARAJ)
JUDGE
